NOTES:

These two films have been incorporated into the German Russian collection of 
films held at GRHS Headquarters in Bismarck ND.  Copies can be obtained from the 
National Archives.

There exists far too much data on these films to completely translate 
everything.  Many of the village items were included in this project - but many 
non-village list items on these films were not extracted  -  but would be of 
interest to the German Russian community if somebody desires to translate 
important portions.

*********************************

The following is the description from the front of film 635;
          (film 636 is similar so not repeated here)

THE AMERICAN HISTORICAL ASSOCIATION
COMMITTEE FOR THE STUDY OF WAR DOCUMENTS
And
THE NATIONAL ARCHIVES AND RECORDS SERVICE
(National Archives Microcopy No. T-81 Roll No. 635)

Collection:  World War II Records Division  Serial: 853 Record Group No. 1035 
             Frames: 5435410-5436753 Item:   DAI 1147, 1149, 1150/1

Provenance:  Deutsches Ausland Institute 
Filmed: x throughout Forschungsstelle des Russland-Deutschtums selectively

Notes:
     DAI 1146 (on Serial 853).
     DAI 1147 (begins with frame 5435410).  Two folders of FSt Russlddtm
               containing its correspondence with DAI' Haupt Abt. Sippenkunde 
               and Volksforschung, 1938-39, 1939-41, respectively.
     DAI 1149 (begins with frame 5435984).  Folder of FSt. Russlddtm containing
              it correspondence with DAI on miscellaneous subjects, 1936-43.
     DAI 1150/1 (begins with frame 54366295).  (DAI 1150/2,3 on Serial 854)
                DAI folders, probably of FSt Russlddtm, "Deutsche Doerfer 
               =Russland," containing statistical information on the population 
               of German settlements in Russia, descriptions of such towns, 
               historical information on them, lists of Russian Germans killed 
               in World War I (in each town), etc. compiled 1931.  The folders 
               are alphabetized according to town names: A-H, J-R, S-Z, 
               respectively.

                    Liebel, 1959 

Notes;
FSt Russlddtm = research center of (for?) the German/Russians
Deutsche Doerfer=German villages
Haupt Abt.= main department
Sippenkunde=Genealogy
Volksforschung=("peoples") research
Forschungsstelle=research center
Last, First Name = last name
born/age         = born date or age or village
Fm               = Family Members
Ch               = Children
Village          = Village (see codified  list above)
flm              = "film number (DAI 635, or 636 etc.)"
page             = page number on film
IN               = Item Number on film
Remarks          = Remarks or misc information
Occupation       = Occupation or Profession
